- **research-lint**: Health-check a research directory produced by /research. Runs seven checks — orphan sources, missing entity/concept hubs, missing comparison candidates, broken wikilinks, stale claims, contradictions, and open-question synthesis. Outputs a report; edits where safe (broken-link flags, open-question append, contradiction surfacing); flags-only otherwise. Always user-triggered, never automated. Trigger when the user says things like "lint my research", "health check my research", "check the wiki", "audit my research dir", "what's wrong with my wiki", "find orphans / contradictions / stale claims… ([iusztinpaul/ai-research-os-workshop](https://github.com/iusztinpaul/ai-research-os-workshop/tree/HEAD/plugins/ai-research-os/skills/research-lint) · ★184)

- **research-mastery**: 信息搜索、整理与验证的综合方法。当需要搜索最新信息、行业调研、技术选型、新闻追踪、事实核查、多源验证、信息整理、摘要生成、知识图谱构建、快速学习新领域、竞品情报收集、数据驱动决策时使用。触发词：搜索、查一下、最新、调研、整理给我、资料、来源、真假、靠谱吗。 ([mike22890/opencode-skills-cn](https://github.com/mike22890/opencode-skills-cn/tree/HEAD/skills/research-mastery) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **research-matching**: Match participants and allocate teams, projects, or reviews with the installed Roth CLI. Use for two-sided one-to-one or capacity-constrained matching, joint team formation and project assignment, reviewer allocation with conflicts and workloads, preference collection, or organizer what-if comparisons. Collective voting and evaluating a manuscript's content use other skills. ([expectedparrot/ep-ra](https://github.com/expectedparrot/ep-ra/tree/HEAD/research_agent/skills/research-matching))

- **research-mcp**: Local MCP aggregator (Zotero + ZotSeek + PaperKit + pp-scite) exposed to ChatGPT and other MCP clients through a private OpenAI tunnel or Cloudflare OAuth. Use when connecting a remote MCP client to Scite, private Zotero, or ZotSeek search, or when the user says my-research-library / research-mcp / ChatGPT research connector. ([dahliasan/researchskills](https://github.com/dahliasan/researchskills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-mcp) · ★2)
- **research-md-compactor**: Convert long verbose research Markdown into compact reference-grade knowledge artifacts preserving substantive content at field level. Use when user asks to compact, compress, normalize, densify, deduplicate, or make deep-research LLM-context friendly. ([Biskiq/spatial-sketch-editor](https://github.com/Biskiq/spatial-sketch-editor/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/research-md-compactor) · ★1)
- **research-md-maker**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "research a topic", "create a research doc", "explore options for X", "what are the different approaches to X", "write up research on X", or needs a broad landscape scan on any subject. Produces a concise, well-segmented .md research file covering a diverse range of options, approaches, or findings. ([fangdav/pocket-space-plugins](https://github.com/fangdav/pocket-space-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/pocket-space/skills/research-md-maker))

- **research-mechanism-analysis**: 从研究对象的原理推导约束、部署选择、改动效果和可复算公式，并检查理论与实测的边界。用于原理讲不清、公式不可信、结论缺因果支撑的报告。 ([cyl19970726/creator-lab](https://github.com/cyl19970726/creator-lab/tree/HEAD/workbenches/research/.agents/skills/research-mechanism-analysis))
- **research-mechanism-diagnosis**: 对已界定的研究问题、失败实例或反常现象进行机制诊断，建立竞争解释、区分性预测和可干预位置。适用于定位根因、分析为什么失败、确定先查什么；不把合理故事当因果结论，不自动设计完整方法或启动实验。 ([EfficientAI-USTC/research-skills](https://github.com/EfficientAI-USTC/research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-mechanism-diagnosis) · ★1)
- **research-mechanism-innovation**: 算法与系统科研中的机制创新方法。把初始 Idea、已有实验和失败结果转化为值得研究的问题； 通过估计改进空间、拆开现象和有证据的观察构造新的机制、模型或结构，并用鉴别实验检验。 用户要求寻找创新点、设计新机制或新方法、突破已有权衡、解释无收益的实验或推进研究方向时使用。 执行研究实验后、汇报结果前也应使用；结果无收益、准备得出某方向无效的结论时必须使用。 ([Sombrer0-1/MySkills](https://github.com/Sombrer0-1/MySkills/tree/HEAD/deep-research))
